# Skipped-test census

Audited source: `598013532f96006a90c324ae86d79c9e24a9e202`, 22 November 2026. Documentation only; no test, scene, asset, workflow or expectation changed. No Unity tests ran for this census.

The **282 EditMode + 257 PlayMode = 539** skipped identities retained from the earlier successful run can all be matched to source explanations. Most need graphics or local evidence; **ten have a plausible small fixture fix**. This is an attribution from source, not a fresh run or a claim that every runtime reason was recovered.

| Primary explanation for those historical skipped identities | EditMode | PlayMode | Total |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| A — needs graphics, a local bake/source, or local acceptance evidence | 271 | 239 | **510** |
| B — parked on a named fault or deferred placement | 6 | 11 | **17** |
| C — historical/stale subject or selection | 1 | 1 | **2** |
| D — small fixture change worth trying on CI | 4 | 6 | **10** |
| Total | **282** | **257** | **539** |

Within A, 502 identities have a graphics/graphics-evidence prerequisite, four need uncommitted road-floor PNGs, two compare original intake scripts that are deliberately uncommitted, and two are unconditional placeholders for local scene/visual acceptance. Within B, three are Tanker route cases, ten are pre-empted by the cabin door fault dictionary, and four cover deferred placements/seller relocation. C contains the missing legacy Tree01 subject and the historical explicit raster measurement. The complete identity list is below; these are occurrences, including parameter variants, not counts of methods.

## The ten to try first

These ten cases are all in the historical skipped list. The proposals preserve their rules and assertions. They are ranked by useful coverage for a small fixture change, not by a promised pass. None was changed or rerun here.

| Rank | Existing case | Small change and the coverage it restores |
| ---: | --- | --- |
| 1 | `CamperHomeDoorPlayTests.PressingTheKeyFromAcrossTheIsland_BuysNothing` ([S208](#s208)) | Apply the existing focus-independent input setup, deliver the real key and restore settings afterward. Keep the money, ownership and event assertions: a remote purchase must remain impossible. |
| 2 | `CamperHomeDoorPlayTests.WalkingUpToTheDoorAndPressingTheKey_BuysTheCamper_WhichIsStillAShell` ([S209](#s209)) | Use the same input setup; retain the exact debit, ownership, repeat-press and shell-result checks. |
| 3 | `InteractVerbPlayTests.ARealKeyPress_ReachesTheVerb_AndAHeldKeyFiresExactlyOnce` ([S261](#s261)) | Reuse that input setup to exercise actual key dispatch and the held-key edge, rather than skipping before the assertion. |
| 4 | `HelmDashPlayTests.ARealKeyPress_BreaksTheSteerSession_KeysWin` ([S253](#s253)) | Apply the same scoped input setup; preserve the keyboard-over-drag arbitration checks. |
| 5 | `HelmBandPlayTests.TheBandsWheel_SteersTheHull_AndAKeyPressTakesItBack` ([S251](#s251)) | Apply the same scoped input setup; preserve steering and keyboard takeover. |
| 6 | `FishChainModelTests.ZeroAllocationTick_BusyTransfersProcessingAndDemandUseNoManagedBytes` ([S050](#s050)) | Replace the unsupported byte observation with the existing calibrated current-thread allocation-event probe. Keep the busy workload and exact zero-allocation rule. |
| 7 | `TrawlSessionTests.TowingTick_AllocatesZeroBytes_WithPositiveCounterControl` ([S173](#s173)) | Use that probe, retaining the positive/empty controls and all 4,096 accepted towing ticks. Rename the measurement accurately if it reports events instead of bytes. |
| 8 | `LobsterPotCompositionTests.HotPath_AllocatesZeroBytes_WhenCounterPassesPositiveControl` ([S069](#s069)) | Use that probe with the current hot path and unchanged zero-allocation requirement. A failed control must not become allocation proof. |
| 9 | `CarryAnchorHandsTests.WhenTheOtherHandWasNotMeasured_TheGripIsDROPPED_NotMirrored` ([S044](#s044)) | Build a temporary one-hand table; the shipped table now has both hands. Preserve all directions, zero grip offset, requested hand and item-facing assertions. |
| 10 | `NineMileCreekFleetPaintPlayTests.TwoOwnersWakeInTwoDifferentPaints` ([S285](#s285)) | Construct two temporary owners sharing an existing hull mesh with distinct valid schemes. Keep the presentation assertions and the separate shipped-register checks; do not alter the register or its art merely to produce a pair. |

There is already an input precedent in [CameraWheelDeviceTests.cs:34](../../Assets/Tests/PlayMode/CameraWheelDeviceTests.cs#L34): lines 63–67 enable delivery independent of focus, and lines 127–129 restore all three settings. [FixedCameraStopsPlayTests.cs:37](../../Assets/Tests/PlayMode/FixedCameraStopsPlayTests.cs#L37) uses the same approach. This makes the five keyboard proposals concrete, though keyboard delivery still needs a real CI check.

There is already a shared allocation helper, [ManagedAllocationProbe.cs:11](../../Assets/Tests/Support/ManagedAllocationProbe.cs#L11), and [ManagedAllocationProbeTests.cs:7](../../Assets/Tests/EditMode/ManagedAllocationProbeTests.cs#L7). It counts current-thread `GC.Alloc` **events**, calibrates with a retained allocation and an empty scope, and rejects an unavailable recorder. Extend/reuse that coverage; do not add a fourth copied recorder or call event counts bytes. A zero-event result can establish no managed allocation, but cannot provide a positive byte measurement.

## What was counted, and what cannot be reconciled from source alone

The historical reference is [run 45035679830, attempt 1](https://github.com/apholmes7-bot/hiddenharbours/actions/runs/45035679830), head `6aa5ae903f54f3dfb436a49d85960913fed23100`, with artifact `14763792816`. Its previously retained per-case records contain names, classes, outcomes and durations, **not reason messages**. This census uses the names/outcomes as a cross-check and reads the explanations from the pinned source. It did not download or execute another suite, and it does not attribute a recovered XML reason to any case.

The existing [GPU inventory and scheduling proposal](../tooling/gpu-only-tests.md) describes collecting actual per-case and inherited fixture reason messages. That would be the next evidence step for an authoritative runtime-cause manifest; the source attribution here does not replace it or schedule its execution. Respect each fixture's API requirement: the foam-history fixture explicitly requires D3D12, so a generic graphics run is not sufficient for every case.

**The historical arithmetic meets 282 and 257 exactly. The current pinned commit's runtime totals cannot be independently established here.** The two commits differ in 31 tracked test-related files; no workflow difference exists between them. Case-source expansion, setup, imports, graphics support, input focus, live registries and service state decide which branch fires. A source location may affect 126 parameter occurrences, or zero; several locations may apply to the same test. For example, the `FullMeshInteriorRenderTests` graphics helper accounts for 126 historical occurrences, while its two missing-bake branches are additional possible exits from those same methods.

The primary classification uses A for the graphics/local-evidence prerequisite, B for a named fault suppression (even when it needs revalidation), C for an unmessaged assumption or historical/stale reason without a separately named fault, and D for a recoverable fixture/CI prerequisite. A parameterized fault entry can remain in B while its explanation is suspected stale; that uncertainty is spelled out in the fault ledger. The historical classification gives the explicit historical attribute priority over its later graphics guard. It does not add all possible guards together.

The **377 executable source sites** below are a different denominator. They comprise 363 `Assert.Ignore` calls, three `TestCaseData.Ignore` calls, nine `Assume.That` calls, one `Assert.Inconclusive` call and one `[Explicit]` attribute. They reach 821 distinct test methods through direct calls, setup, case sources and shared helpers; that is potential reachability, not an executed case count. Constructors, the road-floor property, custom `TestName` aliases and the shared stage's instance calls were included. The syntax inventory was checked against text searches; comments and assertions that merely mention skipping were excluded.

| Source-site classification | EditMode | PlayMode | Total |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| A | 50 | 65 | **115** |
| B | 3 | 6 | **9** |
| C | 11 | 7 | **18** |
| D | 118 | 117 | **235** |
| All sites | **182** | **195** | **377** |

The large D source count does **not** promise 235 new CI passes. It includes 72 non-editor exits that are inactive in this editor-based workflow, dormant missing-input guards, and prerequisites inside tests that also require graphics. Only ten historical identities are assigned D in the first table. Conditional missing-asset guards are retained in the inventory even when CI already supplies their inputs. Where a genuine new bake or an absent original source is required, that is A, not a request to generate new art in CI.

## Discovery, workflow and result semantics

- [ci.yml:75](../../.github/workflows/ci.yml#L75) selects `ubuntu-latest`. [Lines 276](../../.github/workflows/ci.yml#L276) and [316](../../.github/workflows/ci.yml#L316) request the complete EditMode and PlayMode editor suites with `-batchmode -runTests -testPlatform ...`. There is **no `-testCategory`, `-testFilter`, assembly filter, platform-exclusion argument or added scripting define** in either invocation. The YAML does not itself pass `-nographics`; source guards explicitly expect the CI Null device, and the historical outcomes are consistent with that expectation. Batch mode alone is not proof of the runtime graphics API.
- [ci.yml:107](../../.github/workflows/ci.yml#L107) initially checks out LFS pointers, then [lines 130–142](../../.github/workflows/ci.yml#L130) pull and materialize the real content. A missing import can still trip a conditional guard, but “CI never checks out LFS” is not an explanation. No road-chunk bake or local plate-baseline step is present. Setup failures and skipped workflow *steps* are not skipped NUnit *cases*.
- No `[Ignore]`, `[UnityPlatform]`, `[Platform]`, per-case `Ignore =`/`Explicit =`, or direct throw of an ignore/inconclusive exception was found in tracked test source. One `[Explicit]` exists ([S368](#s368)); three case-source calls construct ignored cases ([S098](#s098), [S099](#s099), [S102](#s102)). No NUnit category attribute was found, and this workflow has no category filter.
- `Assert.Inconclusive` and failed assumptions are not ordinary successful assertions. The [existing result gate](../../tools/ci/results.py#L35) distinguishes `Skipped` from `Inconclusive` and rejects inconclusive results. None of the historical 539 names requires an assumption or inconclusive call as its explanation; these potential exits are inventoried without adding them to that skipped total. Four assumptions have no message: [S049](#s049), [S292](#s292), [S293](#s293), [S294](#s294).
- `#if`/assembly exclusions can remove a test from discovery entirely. They do not create an XML skipped case. All 15 test/support assembly definitions require `UNITY_INCLUDE_TESTS`; the 12 EditMode assemblies and support assembly include only Editor, while both PlayMode assemblies have empty platform lists. The RigSpike assembly additionally requires `HH_JS_RIG_SPIKE`, which [ProjectSettings.asset:687](../../ProjectSettings/ProjectSettings.asset#L687) does not set. Its four test methods are not contributors to the 539; its header describes the extra interpreter binaries and opt-in define needed. The exact assembly and preprocessor inventories follow the source table.
- `UNITY_EDITOR` branches are active in this workflow; their `#else`/`!UNITY_EDITOR` exits are player-build paths. `UNITY_5_3_OR_NEWER` selects the Unity implementation, often the allocation recorder; `HH_STANDALONE_CONTRACT_TESTS` changes JSON/path adapters for an independent host. Neither creates a skipped NUnit record on its own. The four distinct source conditions and all 152 files containing directives are listed below.
- Bare early `return`/`yield break`, an empty case source, a caught exception, a timeout or an editor crash must not be relabelled as an ignored case. [StPetersLandingReview.cs:92](../../Assets/Tests/PlayMode/StPetersLandingReview.cs#L92), for example, catches ignore/inconclusive exceptions for a separate review harness; it is not another skip-producing NUnit site. This census concerns explicit skip/inconclusive paths and discovery exclusions, not every way a test could fail to establish its claim.

Treat that as existing work, not three new un-skipping recommendations. The fuel-stop source named by [S080](#s080) is also tracked despite its “not in the repo yet” reason.

## Full source table

Rows are sorted A, B, C, D, then source path/line. Each row is one executable source location; repeated reasons remain separate rows. “Tests” lists named methods reached through that helper/setup/source; parameterized occurrences are expanded in the historical table. A condition is a necessary enclosing predicate, not a complete control-flow proof: earlier guards, loop data and prior failure can prevent it being reached. `#if` conditions retain their branch polarity. Quoted expressions preserve the source reason, with whitespace collapsed for readability; dynamic `wait`/`why` strings are expanded in the fault ledger above. The linked line is the authoritative spelling.
